# CARD-2082 class inventory

Source pin `771bc1a46afe5893894b050f4da47effb6c758e7`. The [detector](2026-11-26-card-2082-invariant-detector.md) examined 2925 tracked C# sources and 2300 declaring types with direct TUnit tests. 1138 types produced a signal. Four additional fixture controls with zero signals are included for comparison. Harness-only probes under tests/ and linked source under tests/Shared are part of the scan. The non-C# files under tests/ are fixture assets, probe programs or the standalone mutation driver in tests/card-file-privacy-controls.py.

The first two tables are the proposed migration workload. A class in the split table contributes its current-tree assertions and their focused controls. Product cases keep their owning fixtures. The fourth table records types whose detected reads are confined to specific helper sites for scratch Git, root discovery, stub setup, diagnostics or staged binaries. These stay in their product lane on that evidence. The last table retains other lower-confidence candidates from the scan. Each row links the declaration. Rows link one detected member when available. This is a starting point for inspection, including for mixed classes whose other members own the global assertions. The detector emits all partial files, inputs and member locations. Tables show up to three representative path hints unless the input has been identified by hand. A literal in a fixture is a search hit, so candidate rows carry no automatic migration authority.

**Reading the timing columns.** Linux cells give observed passed/failed/skipped cases and their summed TRX seconds for the most recent matching row in the inspected clean-source, verified-build, successful Linux checkpoint reports. The receipt key maps to the run and commit below. A narrow row attests only its reported selection. Results from red reports discussed separately in the plan retain their original verdict. The historical Windows column shows serial seconds, node count and run identifier from entries explicitly marked `measured` in [nightly-class-costs.json](../../../scripts/lib/nightly-class-costs.json). The file contains other model prices which are excluded here. Its measured entries can include failures and different node counts. Columns B and F show Windows evidence from the requested b8cbfb24 and 1fe763a9 respectively. A dash means evidence unavailable, including tests introduced after a historical run. See the [plan](2026-11-26-card-2082-repository-invariants-plan.md) for the native group 113, 141 and 142 sources. Coverage, platform eligibility, whole-group wall and p95 require separate evidence.
